\par Then, letting $J_{i}=\{j\in I\;\big|\;{\mathsf{{q}}}_{j}={\mathsf{{q}}}_{i}\}$ and

$$\qquad\qquad\begin{array}[]{l@{\ =\ }l@{\hspace{2cm}}l@{\ =\ }l}\mathsf{G}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{p}}}}&\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.\mathsf{G}_{i}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{p}}}}}\hfil\hskip 56.9055pt&\mathsf{G}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{q}}}_{i}}&\displaystyle{\sum_{j\in J_{i}}{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}.\mathsf{G}_{j}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{r}}}}}+\displaystyle{\sum_{k\in K_{i}}{\mathsf{G}_{k}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{r}}}}}}\end{array}$$By inductive hypothesis, we know that
\begin{equation}r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}({\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p_{0}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:\mathsf{G}_{h}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{p}}}_{0}},\ldots,{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p_{n}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:\mathsf{G}_{h}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{p}}}_{n}},\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}:{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]})\end{equation}\par By repeatedly applying~\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:runs-preserved-by-extchoice}

$$\frac{\textstyle\rule[-5.59721pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}\frac{\textstyle\rule[-5.59721pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}\frac{\textstyle\rule[-5.59721pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}}{\textstyle\rule[-2.15277pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}(\Delta^{\prime})}}{\textstyle\rule[-2.15277pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}\begin{array}[]{l}\langle{{\mathsf{{q}}}_{h}},{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{h}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{h}}\rangle r^{\prime}\ \in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}(\Delta^{\prime}[{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}},\ {{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q_{h}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}}\mapsto{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{h}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]},\ {\mathsf{G}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{q}}}_{h}}}}])\end{array}}{\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTCom_{2}]}$}}}{\textstyle\rule[-2.15277pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}\langle{{\mathsf{{p}}},\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{h}}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{h}},{}\rangle\langle{{\mathsf{{q}}}_{h}},{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{h}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{h}}\rangle r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}(\Delta)}\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTCom_{1}]}$}$$\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Case \mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RGSeq]}$}: Then $\mathsf{G}=\mathsf{G}_{1};\mathsf{G}_{2}$ and $r=r_{1}r_{2}$

\hfill\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{2}[{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{1},\ {\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p_{0}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\mapsto{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{1}{\mathsf{{{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}}}}\,{{\mathsf{{p}}}}_{0}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]},\ \overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{2}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{2}};\ldots;\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{n}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{n}}}])\end{array}}\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTCom_{2}]}$}}{\textstyle\rule[-2.15277pt]{0.0pt}{12.91663pt}r^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{2})}\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTCom_{1}]}$}$$\par We now show that $r^{\prime}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(\mathsf{G}^{*^{f}}_{1})$ implies
$r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{1}^{\star})$.
By inductive hypothesis, $\mathcal{R}(\mathsf{G}_{1})\subseteq\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{1})$.
By \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:aux-conv-recursive}, $\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(\mathsf{G}^{*^{f}})\subseteq\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{1}^{\star})$.
Therefore, $r^{\prime}\in\widetilde{\mathcal{R}}(\mathsf{G}^{*^{f}})$ implies
$r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{1}^{\star})$.
By \mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTSeq]}$},
$r=r^{\prime}r^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{1}^{\star};\Delta_{2})$
since $r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{1}^{\star})$ and
$r^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta_{2})$.
\end{itemize}\end{proof}\par\par\par\par\@@numbered@section{subsection}{toc}{Implementations cover specifications}\begin{defi}A pseudo-type ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}$ is \emph{viable} if either of the
following holds
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@$\mathsf{nf}({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={e}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}$
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@$\mathsf{nf}({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$ or
$\mathsf{nf}({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={\displaystyle{\sum_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$ with
${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}$ viable for all $i\in I$
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@$\mathsf{nf}({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}^{\star};{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ with ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ viable, and either ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ passively
compatible or ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}={\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$.
\end{itemize}A specification $\Delta$ is \emph{viable} when every type in $\Delta$ is
viable.
\end{defi}\par\begin{lem}Let $\mathcal{G}(\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}})$ a global type and $\Delta$ a specification
such that (i) $\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta)}=\{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\;\big|\;{\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})\}$ and (ii) $\Delta:{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\mapsto\mathcal{G}\!\!\upharpoonright\!{{\mathsf{{q}}}}$ for all ${\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$.
Then $\Delta$ is viable.
\end{lem}\par Inwhatfollowswewrite\langle{S},{\sigma}\rangle\xhookrightarrow{E\vdash\widetilde{\alpha}}\langle{S^{\prime}},{\sigma^{\prime}}\rangle withE=e_{1}\land\ldots\land e_{n}and\widetilde{\alpha}=\alpha_{1}\ldots\alpha_{n}forthesequence\langle{S},{\sigma}\rangle\xrightarrow{e_{1}\vdash\alpha_{1}}\langle{S_{2}},{\sigma_{2}}\rangle\xrightarrow{e_{2}\vdash\alpha_{2}}\ldots\xrightarrow{e_{n}\vdash\alpha_{n}}\langle{S^{\prime}},{\sigma}\rangle.Let\mathtt{n}(\alpha)=\mathtt{fy}(\alpha)\cup\mathtt{bn}(\alpha);thedefinitionsof\mathtt{n}(),\mathtt{fy}()and\mathtt{bn}(\_)straightforwardlyextendtosequencesoflabels.\par\par\begin{lem}Let ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}$ be a viable pseudo-type and $e$ such that $\mathsf{nf}({e,{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}}$. Then, there exist $\Gamma$ and $P$ and $\Delta$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}$ such that for any $\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}\supseteq\mathtt{fy}({\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}})$ and
${\mathsf{{p}}}$ it holds that
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma}\ \vdash\ {P}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta,{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}$ and
$\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}}\big)=\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\big)$.
\end{lem}\par\begin{proof}The proof follows by induction on the structure of ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}$.
\par\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}={e_{j}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}$. It follows immediately by using rule
$\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[VEnd]}$}$.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}={\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$. Note that
$\mathsf{nf}({e,{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}}$ implies $\mathsf{nf}({e,{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}})={{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}$ for
all $i\in I$.
Then, by inductive hypothesis there exist $\Gamma_{i}$, $P_{i}$,
$\Delta_{i}$, ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}$ for any $\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}\supseteq\mathtt{fy}({\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i})$
and ${\mathsf{{p}}}$ it holds that
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma_{i}}\ \vdash\ {P_{i}}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{i},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime}}$
and $\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}\big)=\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime}}\big)$.
Then, take $s$ and $x$ fresh, i.e.,
$s\not\in\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta^{\prime}_{0},\ldots,\Delta^{\prime}_{n})}$ and
$x\not\in\mathrm{dom}{(\Gamma^{\prime}_{0},\ldots,\Gamma^{\prime}_{n})}$ and note that
$${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma_{i},x:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}}\ \vdash\ {P_{i}}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{i},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}{s}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{e}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}}$$for any ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}$ and ${{\mathsf{{p^{\prime}}}}}$.
By~\lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{thm:distribution-end-pseudo-types},
$${e\wedge e^{\prime}_{i}}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma_{i},x:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}}\ \vdash\ {P_{i}}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{i}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}{s}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{{e^{\prime}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}}}$$with $e^{\prime}_{i}=x\neq 0\wedge\ldots\wedge x\neq i-1\wedge x=i$
and
$$\begin{array}[]{lcl}\Delta_{i}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}&=&{e\wedge e_{i}^{\prime}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}};\Delta_{i}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\\
{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime}&=&{e\wedge e_{i}^{\prime}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}};{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}\\
{e^{\prime}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}&=&{e\wedge e_{i}^{\prime}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}};{e}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}\end{array}$$By definition of $\mathsf{nf}({\_,\_})$,
${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime}=\mathsf{nf}({e\wedge e_{i}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}})$.
Since $x\not\in{\color[rgb]{0,0,0}\mathtt{fn}({\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}})}$ and $\mathsf{nf}({e,{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}})={{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}}$ we have that
$\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime}}\big)=\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}\big)$.
Moreover, by typing rules~\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[VSend]}$} and~\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[VSeq]}$},
$${e\wedge e_{i}^{\prime}}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma_{i},x:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}}\ \vdash\ {\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\,{c_{i}};P_{i}}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{i}^{\prime\prime}}$$such that $c_{i}$ is some constant of type ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}$ and
$$\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}={e\wedge e_{i}^{\prime}}\Yleft{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}};\Delta_{i}^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}$$Then, take ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}=\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}\Delta^{\prime\prime}_{i}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}$.
Note that ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}$ is well-defined (because the guards in
all types are different) and $\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime}}\big)=\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}}\big)$ because
$\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}^{\prime}}\big)=\Game\big({{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}\big)$ holds for every $i$.
\par Finally, define
$\Delta=\Delta_{0},\ldots,\Delta_{n},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}^{\prime},{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}{s}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p^{\prime}}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}:{e}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}$,
and
$\Delta=\Delta_{0},\ldots,\Delta_{n}$ and
$P={s}(x).~{x=0}~~P_{0}~~{~{x=1}~~P_{1}~~{\ldots}}$.
The proof is concluded by straightforward use of typing rules.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\displaystyle{\sum_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$. It follows
analogously to the previous case.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}^{\star};{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ with ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}$ with
${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}={\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$.
It follows by inductive hypothesis on both ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$
to conclude that there exist $P_{1}$ and $P_{2}$ and by taking $P=~{x}~~{\ell}~~{P_{1}};P_{2}$.
\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}^{\star};{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}$ and ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ passively
compatible. It follows by inductive hypothesis on both ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{1}$
and ${\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{2}$ to conclude that there exist $P_{1}$ and $P_{2}$ and by
taking $P=~P_{1}~~P_{2}$.
\par\end{itemize}\par\end{proof}\par\par\par\begin{lem}If ${}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {}\ \vdash\ {}\ \triangleright\ {}$ and $x\not\in\mathtt{fx}(P)$ then, for all $x^{\prime}\in\mbox{$\mathbb{X}$}$,
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma|_{-x^{\prime}}[{x}\mapsto{\Gamma(x^{\prime})}]}\ \vdash\ {P\{x/x^{\prime}\}}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\prime}}$
where $\Delta^{\prime}|_{\mbox{$\mathbb{U}$}\cup\mbox{$\mathbb{Y}$}}=\Delta|_{\mbox{$\mathbb{U}$}\cup\mbox{$\mathbb{Y}$}}$ and
for all ${\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\in\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta)}$, $\Delta^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}=\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\{x/x^{\prime}\}$.
\end{lem}\par\begin{proof}Trivial, observing that typing does not depend on the identity of
the variables used in processes.
\end{proof}\par\begin{defi}Let ${{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$ be a $\iota$-implementation of a global type $\mathcal{G}$ at
${\color[rgb]{0.7813,0.3828,0.2031}u}$, $e$ a guard, $\Gamma$ an environemnt, and $\Delta$ a
specification.
We say that ${{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$, $\Gamma^{\prime}\supseteq\Gamma$, and specification
$\Delta^{\prime}={(\Delta_{{\mathsf{{q}}}})}_{{\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})}$ are
\emph{compatible with $e$, $\Gamma$, and $\Delta$} if
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@the queue on each $\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}\in\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}$ in ${{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$ is typed
as $\Delta(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})$ and
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@for all ${\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$
\begin{equation}{e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma^{\prime}}\ \vdash\ {\iota({{\mathsf{{q}}}})}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{{\mathsf{{q}}}}}\text{ and }\Game\big({\Delta_{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)=\Game\big({\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)\end{equation}\end{itemize}\end{defi}\par\begin{lem}Let $\mathcal{G}(\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}})$ be a global type, $\Delta$ be a viable
specification such that $\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}\subseteq\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta)}$ and
${\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\in\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta)}$ for all
${\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$, and let ${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma}\ \vdash\ {P}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\prime}}$
be a judgement where
$\Delta^{\prime}{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}=\Delta{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}$ for a
participant ${\mathsf{{p}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$.
For all $r\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta)$ there are ${{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$, an
environment $\Gamma^{\prime}$, and a specification
$\Delta^{\prime}$
compatible with $e$, $\Gamma$, and $\Delta$ such that
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@$\iota({\mathsf{{p}}})=P$
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all
$\sigma\ltimes{(\Delta^{\prime};e;{{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}};\Gamma^{\prime})}$ there is
$r^{\prime}\in\mathcal{R}\langle{{{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}},{\sigma}\rangle$
such that $r\lessdot r^{\prime}$.
\end{enumerate}\end{lem}\par\begin{proof}By induction on the structure of the derivation
$r\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta)$.
In the proof, ${}\ {}\ {\iota(\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}})}{}{\Delta(\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}})}$
shortens ${}\ {}\ {{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{\mathsf{v}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}}{}{\Delta(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})}$ for all
$\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}\in\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}$ where ${\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{\mathsf{v}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}$ is the queue
on $\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}$ in ${{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$.
\par\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTEnd]}$} Then, $r=\epsilon$ and for all
${\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$,
$\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}={e_{{\mathsf{{q}}}}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}$ and
$\Delta(\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}})={}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}$.
Now we show that each condition holds.
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@Take $\iota({\mathsf{{p}}})=P$, and $\iota({\mathsf{{q}}})=$ for
all ${\mathsf{{q}}}\neq{\mathsf{{p}}}$, and
${}\ {}\ {\iota(\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}})}{}{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Let $\Gamma^{\prime}=\Gamma$, $\Delta_{{\mathsf{{p}}}}=\Delta^{\prime}$ and
$\Delta_{{\mathsf{{q}}}}:{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\mapsto{e}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}$ for
all ${\mathsf{{q}}}\neq{\mathsf{{p}}}$.
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@Then condition~\eqref{lem-cov-cond-2} holds for
participant ${\mathsf{{p}}}$ by the hypothesis
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {}\ \vdash\ {P}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\prime}=\Delta_{{\mathsf{{p}}}}}$, and
straightforwardly for participants ${\mathsf{{q}}}\neq{\mathsf{{p}}}$
observing that
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma}\ \vdash\ {\iota({{\mathsf{{q}}}})}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{{\mathsf{{q}}}}}$ and guard
removals coincide since all local types are $\mathsf{end}$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@For condition~\eqref{lem-cov-cond-4} it is enough to take
any store $\sigma$ mapping each free variable $x$ of $P$ in a
value of type $\Gamma(x)$ so that $e$ evaluates to $\mathtt{true}$
in $\sigma$ (the existence of such values is guaranteed by
the typing of $P$).
This also entails~\eqref{cons5:eq} of \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{def:consistent}.
Finally, note that
$r^{\prime}=\epsilon\in\mathcal{R}\langle{{{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}},{\sigma}\rangle$ from
\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[REnd]}$} and $r\lessdot r^{\prime}$ follows from
\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[\lessdot\text{-emp}]}$}.
\end{enumerate}\end{enumerate}\par\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@\mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RTCom_{1}]}$} There is ${\mathsf{{q}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$
such that
$\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}={\displaystyle{\bigoplus_{i\in I}{e_{i}}\Yleft{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{i}}\ {{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{i}}.{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{i}}}}$
and $r=\langle{{\mathsf{{q}}}},{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}\rangle r_{1}$ with
$j\in I$ and $r_{1}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta^{\prime\prime})$ where
$\Delta^{\prime\prime}=\Delta[{{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})},\ \vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}\mapsto{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{j},\ {}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}}\cdot{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}}]$.
Note that $\Delta^{\prime\prime}$ is viable and that
$\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{q}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}\neq{\mathtt{false}}\Yleft{\mathsf{end}}$, which implies
$\neg(e_{j}\iff\mathtt{false})$.
\par We distinguish two cases.
\par\par\@@unnumbered@section{paragraph}{toc}{Case $\ptp q = \ptp p$} By \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:inv-subj-red},
we know that for any $\sigma_{0}$ such that
$\sigma_{0}\ltimes{(;;P;\Delta^{\prime})}$ it holds that
$\langle{P},{\sigma_{0}}\rangle\xhookrightarrow{E\vdash\widetilde{\beta}}\langle{P^{\prime\prime}},{\sigma_{1}}\rangle\xrightarrow{e^{\prime}\vdash\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}\mathsf{v}}\langle{P^{\prime}},{\sigma_{2}}\rangle$
with ${}\ {}\ {\mathsf{v}}{}{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}$, and
$\mathtt{n}(\widetilde{\beta})\cap\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}=\emptyset$, and
$\neg(e\land E\land e^{\prime}\iff\mathtt{false})$.
By applying \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{thm:sr} to the hypothesis
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma}\ \vdash\ {P}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\prime}}$ and $\sigma_{0}$, we have
\begin{equation}\Delta^{\prime}\xhookrightarrow{\widetilde{\beta}^{\prime}}\Delta^{\prime}_{1}\xrightarrow{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}\Delta^{\prime}_{2},\quad{e\land E\land e^{\prime}}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma^{\prime\prime}}\ \vdash\ {P^{\prime}}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\prime}_{2}},\quad\text{and}\quad\Gamma\subseteq\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\end{equation}(where the sequence $\widetilde{\beta}^{\prime}$ is the sequence $\widetilde{\beta}$
where expressions are replaced by their sorts).
By \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:preservation-of-disjoint-endpoint-types},
$\mathtt{n}(\widetilde{\beta}^{\prime})\cap\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}=\emptyset$ implies
$\Delta^{\prime}_{1}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}=\Delta^{\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}=\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}$ for all ${\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$.
Moreover, $\Delta^{\prime}_{2}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{p}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}={\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathbb{T}}_{j}$ because of the
reduction $\Delta^{\prime}_{1}\xrightarrow{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}\Delta^{\prime}_{2}$.
By inductive hypothesis on
$r_{1}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta^{\prime\prime})$ and~\eqref{eq:lem-impl-covers-local-sent-b}, we know that there are
${{}{\iota^{\texttt{ih}}}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$,
$\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}\supseteq\Gamma^{\prime\prime}$, and
$\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}={(\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}})}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})}$
compatible with $e\land E\land e^{\prime}$, $\Gamma^{\prime\prime}$, and
$\Delta_{2}^{\prime}$.
Namely,
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@$\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({\mathsf{{p}}})=P^{\prime}$ and
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@${e\land E\land e^{\prime}}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}}\ \vdash\ {\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({{\mathsf{{r}}}})}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}}$
and
$\Game\big({\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)=\Game\big({\Delta^{\prime\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)$,
for all ${\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all
$\sigma^{\texttt{ih}}\ltimes{(\Delta^{\texttt{ih}};e\land E\land e^{\prime};{{}{\iota^{\texttt{ih}}}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}};\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}})}$
there is
$r^{\texttt{ih}}_{1}\in\mathcal{R}\langle{{{}{\iota^{\texttt{ih}}}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}},{\sigma^{\texttt{ih}}}\rangle$
such that $r_{1}\lessdot r^{\texttt{ih}}_{1}$.
\end{enumerate}Then, let
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@$\iota({\mathsf{{p}}})=P$, and $\iota({\mathsf{{q}}})=\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({\mathsf{{q}}})$
for all ${\mathsf{{q}}}\neq{\mathsf{{p}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$, and
$\iota(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})={}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{\mathsf{v}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}$ where
$\iota^{\texttt{ih}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})={}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{\mathsf{v}}\cdot\mathsf{v}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}$ (note
that~\eqref{it:iota} and the definition of $\Delta^{\prime\prime}$ imply
that $\iota^{\texttt{ih}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})$ is a non-empty queue); this implies
${}\ {}\ {\iota^{\texttt{ih}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})}{}{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}}\cdot{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}}$
since
${}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}}\cdot{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}=\Delta^{\prime\prime}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})$.
Observe that for all
$\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}\in\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}\setminus\{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}\}$ we have
${}\ {}\ {\iota(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})}{}{\Delta^{\prime\prime}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})}$ since
$\iota^{\texttt{ih}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})=\iota(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})$ and
$\Delta(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})=\Delta^{\prime\prime}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}})$.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Note that $\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}\supseteq\Gamma$ since
$\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}\supseteq\Gamma^{\prime\prime}$ and
$\Gamma^{\prime\prime}\supseteq\Gamma$ by~\eqref{eq:lem-impl-covers-local-sent-b}.
Let $\Delta_{{\mathsf{{p}}}}=\Delta^{\prime}$ and
$\Delta_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}=\Delta_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}^{\prime}$ for all
${\mathsf{{r}}}\neq{\mathsf{{p}}}$; then
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@by the validity of ${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {}\ \vdash\ {}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\prime}}$ and the fact
that $\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}\supseteq\Gamma$, we have
${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}}\ \vdash\ {\iota({\mathsf{{p}}})}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{{\mathsf{{p}}}}}$
applying \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:typing-weakening}.
For ${\mathsf{{r}}}\neq{\mathsf{{p}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$, the validity
of the judgement in~\eqref{lem-cov-cond-1-HI-send} implies
the validity of ${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {}\ \vdash\ {\iota({\mathsf{{r}}})}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}}$
because $\mathrm{var}({E\land e^{\prime}})$ are not bound in
$\iota({\mathsf{{r}}})$.
Also,
$\Game\big({\Delta_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)=\Game\big({\Delta^{\prime\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)=\Game\big({\Delta{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)$
where the first equality holds by the inductive hypothesis
and the second by~\eqref{lem-cov-cond-1-HI-send}.
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@Let $\sigma$ be $\sigma^{\texttt{ih}}$ restricted on
$\bigcup_{{\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})}\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}|_{\mbox{$\mathbb{Y}$}})}\cup\mathrm{dom}{(\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}})}$.
Then the first three conditions of \lx@cref{creftype\lx@tilde refnum}{def:consistent} are
trivially satisfied, $e$ evaluates to $\mathtt{true}$ in $\sigma$
since $\sigma|_{\mathrm{var}({e})}=\sigma^{\texttt{ih}}|_{\mathrm{var}({e})}$, and likewise
for the last condition of consistency.
\end{enumerate}\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\itemize@item@Note that
$r=\langle{{\mathsf{{p}}}},{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}\rangle r_{1}\lessdot\langle{{\mathsf{{p}}}},{\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}\rangle r^{\texttt{ih}}_{1}=r^{\prime}$
follows from \mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[\lessdot\text{-cmp}]}$} and~\eqref{lem-cov-cond-4-HI-send}.
The fact that $r^{\prime}\in{{}{}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$ follows by repeated applications
of rule \mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RExt]}$} (once for any $\beta\in\widetilde{\beta}$)
followed by an application of \mbox{\footnotesize$\mathsf{[RSnd]}$}.
\end{itemize}\par\par\par\@@unnumbered@section{paragraph}{toc}{Case $\ptp q \neq\ptp p$}
By inductive hypothesis on
$r_{1}\in\mathcal{R}_{\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}}(\Delta^{\prime\prime})$ and~\eqref{eq:lem-impl-covers-local-sent-b}, we know that there are
${{}{\iota^{\texttt{ih}}}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}$,
$\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}\supseteq\Gamma^{\prime\prime}$, and
$\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}={(\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}})}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})}$
compatible with $e\land E\land e^{\prime}$, $\Gamma^{\prime\prime}$, and
$\Delta_{2}^{\prime}$.
Namely,
\begin{enumerate}\enumerate@item@$\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({\mathsf{{p}}})=P$
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@${e}\ \text{\textvisiblespace}\ {\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}}}\ \vdash\ {\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({{\mathsf{{r}}}})}\ \triangleright\ {\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}}$
and
$\Game\big({\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)=\Game\big({\Delta^{\prime\prime}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2}(}\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2},}{{\mathsf{{r}}}}{\color[rgb]{0.2,0,0.2})}}\big)$,
for all ${\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})$
\par\vskip 4.0pt\vskip 4.0pt plus 2.0pt minus 1.0pt\enumerate@item@for all store
$\sigma^{\texttt{ih}}\ltimes{(\bigcup_{{\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})}\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}};e;{{}{\iota^{\texttt{ih}}}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}};\Gamma^{\texttt{ih}})}$
there is
$r^{\texttt{ih}}_{1}\in\mathcal{R}\langle{{{}{\iota^{\texttt{ih}}}_{{}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}@}{}}}},{\sigma^{\texttt{ih}}}\rangle$
such that $r_{1}\lessdot r^{\texttt{ih}}_{1}$.
\end{enumerate}Then, let
\begin{itemize}\itemize@item@$\iota(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})={}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{\mathsf{v}}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}$ where
$\iota^{\texttt{ih}}(\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j})={}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}[}\vec{\mathsf{v}}\cdot\mathsf{v}{\color[rgb]{0.3,0.3,0.3}]}$ (the proof that queues $\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}\in\vec{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}}$ are typed
by $\Delta^{\prime\prime}$ is as in the previous case ${\mathsf{{p}}}={\mathsf{{q}}}$) and
$$\iota:\begin{cases}{\mathsf{{p}}}\mapsto P\\
{\mathsf{{r}}}\mapsto\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({\mathsf{{r}}})&\forall{\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})\setminus\{{\mathsf{{p}}},{\mathsf{{q}}}\}\\
{\mathsf{{q}}}\mapsto{s}(x).~{x}~~\overline{\mathsf{{\color[rgb]{0.125,0.1719,0.75}y}}_{j}}\,{\mathsf{v}};\iota^{\texttt{ih}}({\mathsf{{q}}})~~{Q}\end{cases}$$with $s$ and $x$ fresh (formally,
$s\not\in\bigcup_{{\mathsf{{r}}}\in\mathcal{P}(\mathcal{G})}\mathrm{dom}{(\Delta^{\texttt{ih}}_{{\mathsf{{r}}}})}$, and
$x\not\in\mathrm{dom}{(\Gamma)}$, and ${}\ {}\ {\mathsf{v}}{}{{\color[rgb]{0.0586,0.543,0.4883}\mathsf{d}}_{j}}$) and $Q$
a process such that the following judgement holds:

$\{{\mathsf{{p}}}_{1},\ldots,{\mathsf{{p}}}_{n}\}$. $A_{i}$ is a whole-spectrum
realisation of ${\mathsf{{p}}}_{i}\in P$ in conversation protocol
$R=\langle(P,M),A\rangle$ if for all $w\in L(R)$ there exist
${\{A_{j}\}}_{j\in\{1,\ldots,n\}\setminus\{i\}}$ such that $w\in C(<(P,M),A_{1},\ldots,A_{n}>)$.
\end{defi}\par\begin{defi}$A_{i}$ is a WSI of ${\mathsf{{p}}}_{i}$ in conversation protocol
$R=\langle(P,M),A\rangle$ if: (1) $A_{i}$ is a deterministic guarded
automaton, and (2) $A_{i}$ is a whole-spectrum realisation of
${\mathsf{{p}}}_{i}$ in $R=\langle(P,M),A\rangle$.
